function hasPathSum(root, targetSum):
    return dfs(root,targetSum, 0)


function dfs(node, targetSum, currentSum):
    if node === null:
        return false
    currentSum += node.val
    if node.left === null and node.right === null:
        return targetSum === currentSum
    
    leftNode = dfs(node.left, targetSum, currentSum)
    rightNode = dfs(node.right, targetSum, currentSum)
    
    return leftNode.val || rightNode.val
